# Deep UI sweep — 2026-11-03

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-03-deep-ui-sweep.md`. Harness: `tools/uisweep/` (`npm run uisweep -- --area <a> --vp desktop|phone|phone-land --skin <id>`).
Grid: **poms rebuild** (writes only where a row says so, cleaned up). poms grid read-only (writes blocked) for performance.
Desktop first, the phone in every area (user, 14:13).

## Rubric

- **works** — did what its label says, no page error. `nothing` = the tap changed no DOM, URL or focus, opened nothing and sent nothing: a dead control.
- **reach** — on screen, hit-testable (nothing covers it), phone tap target ≥ 24px (✅ ≥ 32px), a touch path for anything hover-only, no sideways scroll.
- **a11y** — axe (wcag2a/aa/22aa) serious+critical = 0 inside what opened; labelled (icon buttons carry aria-label/title); Escape closes; focus returns.
- **clear** — the label says what it does, no jargon (occurrence/module/ids…, except the operations editor), an empty state says what to do next, a destructive action confirms or undoes; the area's common task in ≤ 3 taps.
- **fast** — tap → painted: menus ≤ 100 ms desktop / ≤ 250 ms phone at 4× CPU; scroll ≤ 150 ms/step median at 4×; no freeze > 3 s after rows show.

Marks: ✅ · 🛠 fixed (commit) · ⚠ left (why) · ✗ broken.

## Baseline

axe (wcag2a/aa/22aa) over poms rebuild after load, before any interaction (`tools/uisweep/_axebase.mjs`):

| | critical | serious | rules (serious+critical) |
|---|---|---|---|
| desktop 1440 | 74 | 25 | button-name ×69 · aria-required-children ×4 (`div[aria-label="Intellectual items"]`) · meta-viewport ×1 · aria-input-field-name ×7 (doc editors) · target-size ×18 (`Read full message`) |
| phone 390 | 73 | 4 | button-name ×68 (the multi-select field pill's `role=combobox` trigger) · aria-required-children ×4 · meta-viewport ×1 · aria-input-field-name ×1 · target-size ×3 (`Show notes`) |

No sideways scroll at either size.
